0db5458 (Actions 38023059329, PBS218318, dgx007/dgx027, 5m17s, Exit 1)
did not run FA3. SGLang's automatic attention-backend choice
(server_args _get_default_attn_backend) avoids FA3 when page_size > 1, so
the workers fell back to FlashInfer. The decode worker then failed during
CUDA-graph capture ("Buffer overflow when allocating memory for
batch_prefill_tmp_s ... Increase the workspace buffer size"), and a
prefill worker hit a FlashInfer JIT toolchain error. The one-GPU probe
measured FA3 at page size 64, which is the intended condition.

Pass --attention-backend fa3 together with --page-size on every ASPIRE
worker when page_size is configured. Everything else is unchanged from
0db5458 (P3/D1, decode pool 393216, c72, page size 64, same image).

Validation: bash -n and the full CPU pytest suite with the updated argv.
